Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the computability framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 374. The resulting operator achieves bound O(15/n^5).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on computability structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 374. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 15. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any computability module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 858. The projective resolution has length at most 123 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to computability theory, we decompose the problem into 858 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 123. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
